FEATURED WORK

My Projects

01 · MEMORY

SRAM Cell Design (6T-10T)

Designed and analyzed 6T-10T SRAM cells for stability and circuit behavior. Performed schematic simulation, stability analysis, and physical layout verification through DRC/LVS.

Cadence VirtuosoCMOSSRAM
02 · STANDARD CELL

NOR1X Standard Cell

Implemented and verified a NOR1X standard cell, developing schematic and physical layout with attention to layout correctness and area/performance trade-offs.

Cadence VirtuosoStandard CellDRC/LVS
03 · LAYOUT

Standard Cell Layout Design

Created CMOS layouts for Inverter, NAND, NOR, AO21, and AO22 logic cells, followed by DRC/LVS verification for design-rule compliance and connectivity.

CMOS LayoutInverterNAND / NOR
04 · ANALOG CMOS

5T Amplifier Design

Designed and simulated a 5-transistor amplifier targeting high voltage gain, achieving approximately 40 dB through transistor-level circuit design and analysis.

Analog CMOSCadence Virtuoso~40 dB Gain
